78 /**
79 * @ingroup linalg_lapack
80 * @brief Interface to batched versions of the LAPACK/cuSOLVER `%getrs` routine.
81 *
82 * @details This function solves systems of linear equations
83 * \f[
84 * \mathbf{A}_i \mathbf{X}_i = \mathbf{B}_i \; ,
85 * \f]
86 * for a batch of matrices indexed by \f$ i \in \{ 0, \ldots, N_b - 1 \} \f$. Here, \f$ N_b \f$ is the batch size. See
87 * also nda::lapack::getrs.
88 *
89 * A batch of matrices is just a 3-dimensional array in either nda::C_layout or nda::F_layout. For a Fortran/C layout
90 * array, the last/first dimension indexes the individual matrices such that `A(:,:,i)`/`A(i,:,:)` corresponds to the
91 * \f$ i \f$-th matrix \f$ \mathbf{A}_i \f$ in the batch.
92 *
93 * Depending on the input array types, the function does the following:
94 * - If the input arrays satisfy nda::mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space, it calls cuBLAS's `cublasXgetrsBatched`.
95 * - Otherwise, it simply loops over all matrices in the batch and calls nda::lapack::getrs.
96 *
97 * @note \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ is allowed to be a lazy conjugate expression (see nda::blas_lapack::is_conj_array_expr),
98 * in which case it is required to be in nda::C_layout. Otherwise, it must have nda::F_layout or nda::C_layout. \f$
99 * \mathbf{B} \f$ is required to be in nda::F_layout.
100 *
101 * @tparam A nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayOrConj<3> type.
102 * @tparam B nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<A, 3> type.
103 * @tparam IPIV nda::blas_lapack::PivotArrayFor<A, 2> type.
104 * @param a Input array. The 3-dimensional array \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ containing LU factorized matrices \f$ \mathbf{A}_i
105 * \f$ as computed by nda::lapack::getrf_batch
106 * @param b Input/output array. On entry, the 3-dimensional array \f$ \mathbf{B} \f$ containing the right hand side
107 * matrices \f$ \mathbf{B}_i \f$. On exit, it contains the corresponding solution matrices \f$ \mathbf{X}_i \f$.
108 * @param ipiv Input matrix. The pivot indices from nda::lapack::getrf_batch. If the matrix is in Fortran (C) layout,
109 * the \f$ i \f$-th column (row) contains the pivot indices from the factorization of \f$ \mathbf{A}_i \f$.
110 * @return Integer return code from the batched LAPACK/cuBLAS call(s). If zero, all calls were successful.
111 */
112 template <BlasArrayOrConj<3> A, BlasArrayFor<A, 3> B, PivotArrayFor<A, 2> IPIV>
113 requires((has_F_layout<A> or has_C_layout<A>) and has_F_layout<B> and (not is_conj_array_expr<A> or has_C_layout<A>))
114 28 int getrs_batch(A const &a, B &&b, IPIV const &ipiv) { // NOLINT (temporary views are allowed here)
115 28 constexpr bool run_on_device = mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space<A, B, IPIV>;
116
117 // transpose ipiv array/view if necessary
118 8 if constexpr (has_C_layout<IPIV>) return getrs_batch(a, b, transpose(ipiv));
119
120 // transpose A array/view if necessary and call the implementation with the correct cuBLAS op flag
121 20 constexpr char op = (has_C_layout<A> ? (is_conj_array_expr<A> ? 'C' : 'T') : 'N');
122 if constexpr (has_C_layout<A>) {
123 12 return detail::getrs_batch_impl<run_on_device>(transpose(a), std::forward<B>(b), ipiv, op);
124 } else {
125 8 return detail::getrs_batch_impl<run_on_device>(a, std::forward<B>(b), ipiv, op);
126 }
127 }

129 /**
130 * @ingroup linalg_lapack
131 * @brief Generic-friendly overload of nda::lapack::getrs for batches stored as 3-dimensional arrays.
132 *
133 * @details It simply calls nda::lapack::getrs_batch and lets generic code call `%getrs(...)` regardless of whether
134 * the input is a single matrix (rank 2) or a batch (rank 3).
135 */
136 template <BlasArrayOrConj<3> A, BlasArrayFor<A, 3> B, PivotArrayFor<A, 2> IPIV>
137 requires((has_F_layout<A> or has_C_layout<A>) and has_F_layout<B> and (not is_conj_array_expr<A> or has_C_layout<A>))
138 int getrs(A const &a, B &&b, IPIV const &ipiv) { // NOLINT (temporary views are allowed here)
139 return getrs_batch(a, std::forward<B>(b), ipiv);
140 }
